## [SFR-PHOTOSHOP-DEEP-DELTA] Photoshop Deep Feature Delta

```text
Deep feature/tool delta below the help-TOC leaf level captured in 06-photoshop-leaf-index.md and 39-photoshop-source-distilled-feature-rows.md.
One record per tool, menu command, filter, blend mode, layer-style effect, panel, Camera Raw control group, automation surface, dialog, or preference category.
Verification semantics: VERIFIED = source page fetched and inspected in this pass; PARTIAL = feature named by the official help TOC/leaf page title or an inspected overview page, but the specific leaf body was not re-fetched; UNVERIFIED = search-snippet or uninspected-source basis.
Vendor names are research/provenance references only and never future Studio tool names. Cloud/AI provider dependencies are stated in app_behavior so local-first Studio planning can route them to adapter or omitted posture.
```

### [SFR-PHOTOSHOP-DEEP-DELTA.smart-and-linked] Smart Objects, Artboards, Frames, and Linked Assets

    name: "Accessibility surface (UI font size, scaling, dynamic shortcuts, screen readers)"
    record_role: "feature_deep_delta"
    app_behavior: "Photoshop's accessibility surface is small and mostly lives under Preferences > Interface: UI Font Size plus Scale UI To Font resize the whole interface for low-vision users, and dynamic/customizable keyboard shortcuts (Edit > Keyboard Shortcuts) allow mouse-free operation. Screen-reader support is OS-level rather than an in-app setting: interface elements and menus are exposed to JAWS/NVDA (Windows) and VoiceOver (macOS). There is no dedicated Accessibility preference pane; posture is diagnostics/interactive support of assistive tech, not a feature-rich a11y panel."
    primitive_domain: "diagnostics"
    dedupe_status: "new_surface"

sources:
  - id: PSDD-S01
    url: "https://helpx.adobe.com/photoshop/desktop/make-selections/get-started-selections/selection-tools-overview.html"
    note: "Official selection tools overview; enumerates Move, marquee, lasso, quick selection, magic wand, and object selection tools. Fetched 2026-07-09 via Jina Reader browser engine."
  - id: PSDD-S02
    url: "https://helpx.adobe.com/photoshop/desktop/repair-retouch/remove-objects-fill-space/retouch-tools-overview.html"
    note: "Official retouch tools overview; enumerates healing, eraser, blur/sharpen/smudge, and dodge/burn/sponge tools. Fetched 2026-07-09."
  - id: PSDD-S03
    url: "https://helpx.adobe.com/photoshop/desktop/apply-painting-techniques/fill-objects-selections-layers/painting-tools-overview.html"
